- **clustering-concordance-adjusted-rand-index**: Use when independent cluster assignments of the same cells exist (e.g., CNA-derived and mutation-derived) and an orthogonal-validation metric is needed to confirm a caller's subclone structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-concordance-adjusted-rand-index))
- **clustering-concordance-metric-computation**: Use when you need to quantitatively benchmark whether a feature-quantification method (e.g., MATES vs scTE/SoloTE) yields better downstream cell clustering, given known cell labels and repeated clusterings over multiple seeds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-concordance-metric-computation))
- **clustering-consistency-metric-evaluation**: Use when an imputation method (e.g., Bfimpute, scImpute, MAGIC, SAVER, VIPER, PBLR, DrImpute) must be benchmarked on data with known cell type labels, such as splatter-simulated matrices or labeled 10x datasets, to quantify clustering recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-consistency-metric-evaluation))
- **clustering-cutoff-parameter-sensitivity-analysis**: Decision support for choosing a clustering threshold or alignment mode (e.g., BiG-SCAPE 0.30 vs 0.50, global vs glocal) when an independent validation signal such as known metabolite-GCF pairs is available to score each parameter combination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-cutoff-parameter-sensitivity-analysis))

- **clustering-method-benchmark-comparison**: Use when two protein clustering methods (e.g. Snekmer AAR-kmer clustering vs MMseqs2 easy-cluster) have been run on the same protein FASTA set and you must benchmark them via V-scores and quantify overlap/union coverage of assigned proteins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-method-benchmark-comparison))
- **clustering-method-benchmarking**: Use when a single-cell gene expression matrix must be clustered and compared across multiple clustering methods, or when reproducing scAce's ARI/NMI benchmark (Fig. 2A simulated, Fig. 3A real). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-method-benchmarking))
- **clustering-method-variant-comparison**: Use when scRNA-Seq cells must be assigned to clusters for cell type identification and no single clustering method can be trusted a priori; run four SINCERA clustering variants on the identical input and pick the scheme supported by permutation tests and selective marker expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-method-variant-comparison))
- **clustering-methodology**: This skill should be used when the intelligence-cluster agent needs guidance on clustering intelligence cards to themes, detecting new themes, and determining card-theme assignments. ([cyberstrat-forge/cyber-nexus](https://github.com/cyberstrat-forge/cyber-nexus/tree/HEAD/plugins/market-radar/skills/clustering-methodology) · ★3)
- **clustering-metric-benchmarking**: Decision support for tuning the number of neighbors (k_cutoff) used in SSGATE's Cal_Nbrs_Net graph construction on single-cell multi-omics (h5ad) data, where the goal is to reproduce or validate the hyperparameter sensitivity table (Table 2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-metric-benchmarking))
- **clustering-metric-computation**: Use when comparing cell-cluster assignments produced by two or more scRNA-seq clustering methods against ground-truth cell-type labels and need ARI, NMI and V-measure scores to rank methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-asw-kbet**: Use when comparing batch-integration methods on annotated scRNA-seq AnnData with 'batch_indices' and 'cell_types' columns and you need quantitative ARI/ASW/kBET scores to judge biology preservation vs. batch over-correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-asw-kbet))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-lisi-db**: Use when comparing cell-state clustering quality of methods (e.g., ICAT vs Seurat/Scanorama/Harmony integration) on scRNA-seq perturbation datasets that have ground-truth cell labels and treatment conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-lisi-db))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-purity-homogeneity**: Use when comparing integration methods (e.g., SSGATE, MOFA+, Seurat v5, totalVI, scMM) on paired transcriptome-proteome single-cell data via Leiden clustering, or when reproducing noise-robustness rankings under degraded data quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-purity-homogeneity))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-vmeasure**: Use when comparing clustering assignments of scRNA-seq cells against ground-truth labels (e.g., FACS-sorted CD4+/CD8+ T cells or Tabula Muris Senis cell types) and reporting ARI, NMI, and V-measure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-vmeasure))
- **clustering-metric-computation-nmi-ari-asw**: Use when evaluating predicted single-cell cluster assignments against ground-truth cell type labels, or comparing learned cell embeddings of scMGCA vs baselines (scziDesk, scDeepCluster, DCA, DEC, DESC, scVI, scCAEs, scGNN, scGAE, GraphSCC, Seurat, SHARP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-nmi-ari-asw))
- **clustering-metric-computation-vs-true-labels**: Use when scRNA-seq cell embeddings have been produced by scGCL or one of its ablation variants (task=clustering, epochs=300) and you need agreement metrics against ground-truth cell labels to compare variants across datasets like Adam and Romanov. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-vs-true-labels))
- **clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-nmi**: Use when comparing clustering quality of translated cross-modality profiles (e.g., scButterfly-B vs scButterfly-T vs scButterfly-C on MCC scRNA–scATAC data) against true cell-type labels to quantify translation fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-nmi))
- **clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-silhouette**: Use when imputed or denoised scRNA-Seq count matrices must be scored for cell-type recovery against known simulated groups (e.g., the six Splatter-simulated groups of Dataset 1) using ASW, ARI, MSE, and zero-recovery metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-silhouette))
- **clustering-metric-evaluation-nmi-ca-ari**: Use when comparing clustering results (e.g., scDCC vs. SC3, CIDR, PhenoGraph, k-means) on labeled scRNA-seq data, or when verifying that added pairwise constraints actually improve NMI/CA/ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-evaluation-nmi-ca-ari))
- **clustering-metrics-computation**: Use when when you have executed multiple clustering tools on the same tandem-MS dataset and need to quantitatively compare their performance using normalized, comparable metrics rather than raw cluster assignments alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metrics-computation) · 2 copies)

- **clustering-parameter-sweep-evaluation**: Use when you need to choose or characterize a weighting parameter for hybrid clone/transcriptome clustering by measuring chance-corrected agreement (Cohen's kappa) between clusters and clonal barcodes across a parameter range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-parameter-sweep-evaluation))
- **clustering-performance-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify whether adding noisy pairwise constraints improves scRNA-seq clustering — e.g., testing robustness of scDCC to 5% or 10% mislabeled must-link/cannot-link constraints versus an unsupervised baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-benchmarking))
- **clustering-performance-evaluation-ari-nmi**: Use when you have per-cell cluster predictions from one or more single-cell clustering methods plus reference cell-type labels, and need to quantify and significance-test clustering accuracy (ARI/NMI). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-evaluation-ari-nmi))
- **clustering-performance-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating scRNA-seq clustering outputs against known cell-type labels, especially when measuring how clustering quality (NMI, CA, ARI) changes as the number of pairwise must-link/cannot-link constraints increases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-metric-computation))
- **clustering-performance-metric-visualization**: Use when comparing clustering outputs (e.g., ICAT sslouvain assignments vs. Seurat/Scanorama/Harmony/no-integration Louvain) across simulated perturbation-severity, marker-gene-count, or cell-proportion conditions against known ground-truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-metric-visualization))
- **clustering-performance-metrics-evaluation**: Use when a multi-clustering integration result (scTriangulate clusters) must be quantitatively compared against silver-standard references or individual modality/resolution clustering solutions using V-Measure, Homogeneity, and Completeness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-metrics-evaluation))

- **clustering-purity-and-n10-evaluation**: Use when you have run one or more MS/MS clustering tools (MS-Cluster, Falcon, MaRaCluster) on a metabolomics or proteomics dataset and need to quantify clustering quality in terms of purity and N10 completeness without relying on FDR-controlled identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-purity-and-n10-evaluation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when you have cluster labels assigned over millions of MS/MS spectra together with peptide identifications and need to quantify clustered fraction, incorrect clustering, and completeness for tool comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when scoring simulated scRNA-seq datasets that carry ground-truth cell-group labels against clustering-quality metrics (ASW, Dunn, Connectivity, DB, CDI) to rank simulation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when comparing two or more cell-by-embedding matrices of the same single-cell dataset against identical cell-type labels — e.g., benchmarking a 137-GEP dimension reduction against PCA, scDHA, or scMGCA embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-evaluation**: Use when you have run SCA on a single-cell counts matrix (e.g., setA PBMC clusters 1-5) and need to judge which clusters the autoencoder encoding reliably reconstructs, especially clusters whose QCM/QCC scores sit near the 0.5 significance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-evaluation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-tabulation**: Decision support for choosing a spectrum clustering tool when you have cluster assignments from multiple tools plus peptide-labeled PSMs (e.g., 1% FDR) and need to compare cluster size distributions, purity, completeness, and data-volume reduction at a comparable error rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-tabulation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-tabulation**: Decision support for evaluating whether synthetic single-cell RNA-seq data from small pilot studies preserve clustering structure — e.g., when comparing DBI/ARI variability of scDBM, scVI prior, scVI posterior, and noise baselines across pilot sizes or technologies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-tabulation))
- **clustering-reproducibility-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking clustering robustness of scATAC-seq methods across sequencing depths or cell counts — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/clustering-reproducibility-evaluation))
- **clustering-result-count-validation**: Use when a spectral networking/clustering step has produced a spectrum-to-cluster assignment and you must verify the number of distinct clusters (e.g. 8,071 families from ~10M spectra) before downstream interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-result-count-validation))
- **clustering-result-reproducibility-checking**: Use when a task requires re-running an unsupervised clustering pipeline (RCC) on published bulk RNA-seq matrices (e.g., Ivy GAP, GTEx, TCGA SKCM) to confirm that reported novel subtypes and cluster counts can be reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-result-reproducibility-checking))
- **clustering-robustness-subset-resampling**: Use when you need to quantify how sensitive a scRNA-seq clustering method is to cell subsampling: run each method on 10 random 95%-cell subsets, compute ARI/NMI per repeat against ground truth, and compare per-method medians. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-robustness-subset-resampling))
- **clustering-seed-selection-strategy**: Use when clustering single-cell (e.g., PBMC scRNA-seq) data on a KNN/adaptive-kernel affinity matrix and a deterministic, assumption-light label-propagation procedure is needed that guarantees every vertex receives exactly one cluster label without a predefined cluster count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-seed-selection-strategy))
- **clustering-selection-criteria-ranking**: Use when many candidate clusterings of a trajectory-containing single-cell dataset must be ranked and compared by selection criteria (connectivity+VRC, ASW, VRC, random) to pick clusterings for MST-based trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-selection-criteria-ranking))
- **clustering-selection-for-trajectory-inference**: Use when a Totem-style trajectory pipeline has produced thousands of candidate clusterings and you must automatically pick which clustering(s) to feed into Slingshot MST construction, rather than hand-picking a biologically sensible model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-selection-for-trajectory-inference))
- **clustering-semantic-ideas**: Use when clustering semantic ideas within creativity lifecycle boundaries, especially when does the artifact make the owned decision for clustering semantic ideas explicit and bounded?. ([Nolane-x/forge-os](https://github.com/Nolane-x/forge-os/tree/HEAD/skills-v2/l1/creativity/clustering-semantic-ideas) · ★10)
- **clustering-semantic-ideas**: Use when clustering semantic ideas is required during creativity work, especially when the result must be traceable, independently reviewable, and safe to hand to another agent. ([Nolane-x/forge-os](https://github.com/Nolane-x/forge-os/tree/HEAD/skills/core/creativity/clustering-semantic-ideas) · ★10)
- **clustering-sensitivity-runtime-tradeoff-analysis**: Use when clustering large protein sequence sets at ~50% sequence identity and Linclust's default m=20 k-mers per sequence yields more (smaller) clusters than a reference tool like UCLUST, prompting a test of whether --kmer-per-seq 80 closes the sensitivity gap at acceptable runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-sensitivity-runtime-tradeoff-analysis))
- **clustering-separation-benchmarking-across-methods**: Use when two or more scATAC-seq embedding/clustering methods must be compared on the same cells and you need a quantitative separation metric (connectivity index) rather than eyeballing t-SNE overlays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/clustering-separation-benchmarking-across-methods))
- **clustering-silhouette-assessment**: Use when integration quality of uniPort-embedded scATAC/scRNA (or other single-cell) datasets must be quantified by Silhouette plus ARI, NMI and F1 to compare modality-linking or cell-type-removal scenarios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-silhouette-assessment))
- **clustering-silhouette-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking feature-level normalization of MTI data (e.g., PRAD-CyCIF .pkl/.h5ad) and you need average silhouette scores, alongside kBET, to judge clustering consistency of raw vs. normalized datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/clustering-silhouette-evaluation))
- **clustering-similarity-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking a dimensionality reduction or clustering variant on scRNA-seq embeddings and you need quantitative agreement scores (ECS, kNN-overlap) against ground-truth labels or a reference method's embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-similarity-metric-computation))
